### Join the Party!

Bring your friends to a *Eventide* Launch Party near you. Play, trade, hang out, have fun, and win cool prizes\*.

### When

Parties will be held Friday: July 25th or Saturday: July 26th or Sunday: July 27th. 

<table>&#10;  <tbody><tr>&#10;    <td>&#10;      <img src="http://www.wizards.com/Magic/Magazine/magic/images/tournamentcenter/2008/eventide_launch.jpg">&#10;    </td>&#10;  </tr>&#10;  <tr>&#10;    <td>Yours for participating, while supplies last.</td>&#10;  </tr>&#10;</tbody></table>

### Locations

*   [Midnight Madness](#mm) (United States and Canada)
*   [United States](#us)
*   [Canada](#canada)

### Play Structure/Format/Product Mix

*   The structure for League play is Sealed Deck. Each participant receives one *Shadowmoor* tournament pack and two *Eventide* boosters to be used to constructed a 40-card minimum league deck. Players may purchase an additional *Eventide* booster after fifth loss and again after tenth loss and add it to their League Deck.
*   The Release Tournament is Sealed Deck and is sanctioned by the DCI. Each participant receives one *Shadowmoor* tournament pack and two *Eventide* boosters to be used to constructed a 40-card minimum tournament deck. The tournament should use the Swiss-Style pairing system with optional cut to single elimination.

### Entry Fee

*   League Play – The entry fee should include the cost of operations and prize product.
*   Release Tournament – The entry fee should include the cost of operations and prize product.

### Prizes

*   **Door Prizes**: *Eventide* Life Counters will be given out randomly through the day (while supplies last).
*   **League Play**: Each participant receives one foil promo card. The top-4 players with the most wins + losses at the end of league play each win an *Eventide* Deckbox.
*   **Release Tournament**: Each participant receives one foil promo card. The top-4 finishers each win an *Eventide* Deckbox.

Check with your local Launch Party location for specific entry fee, and start time information.

\* With event participation. While supplies last.

**Midnight Madness Locations** 

**Midnight Madness** stores will be open at 12:01AM on Friday, July 25 to sell Eventide! Also, many **Midnight Madness** locations will be running Eventide sanctioned and unsanctioned tournaments starting at midnight - contact your local store for details.
